<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Malik bin Al-Huwairith that: He saw the Prophet (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) raise his hands when praying, when he bowed, when he raised his head from bowing, when he prostrated and when he raised his head from prostrating, until they were in level with the top part of his ears</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1085]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1085" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मालिक बिन हुवैरिष (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के उन्होंने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को नमाज़ में जब आप रुकू करते, और जब रुकू से अपना सर उठाते, और जब सज्दा करते, और जब सज्दे से अपना सर उठाते, रफ़अ-यदैन करते देखा, यहाँ तक के आप अपने दोनों हाथों को अपने दोनों कानों की लौ के बिलमुक़ाबिल कर लेते।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1085]</p>

## Narration 648

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1086)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الْمُثَنَّى، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الأَعْلَى، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا سَعِيدٌ، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ نَصْرِ بْنِ عَاصِمٍ، عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ الْحُوَيْرِثِ، أَنَّهُ رَأَى النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَفَعَ يَدَيْهِ فَذَكَرَ مِثْلَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1086]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1086" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

## Narration 649

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1087)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الْمُثَنَّى، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا مُعَاذُ بْنُ هِشَامٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي أَبِي، عَنْ قَتَادَةَ، عَنْ نَصْرِ بْنِ عَاصِمٍ، عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ الْحُوَيْرِثِ، أَنَّ نَبِيَّ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ إِذَا دَخَلَ فِي الصَّلاَةِ فَذَكَرَ نَحْوَهُ وَزَادَ فِيهِ وَإِذَا رَكَعَ فَعَلَ مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ وَإِذَا رَفَعَ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ الرُّكُوعِ فَعَلَ مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ وَإِذَا رَفَعَ رَأْسَهُ مِنَ السُّجُودِ فَعَلَ مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1087</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i   1087 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from Malik bin Al-Huwairith that: He saw the Prophet of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) raise his hands when he started to pray, and he narrated a similar report and added: &quot;When he bowed he did likewise, and when he raised his head from bowing he did likewise, and when he raised his head from prostration he did likewise</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1087</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i   1087 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मालिक बिन हुवैरिष (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जब नमाज़ में दाख़िल हो जाते... फिर आगे उन्होंने इसी तरह की हदीस ज़िक्र की, इस में उन्होंने यह इज़ाफ़ा किया है के जब आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) रुकू करते तो भी ऐसा ही करते, और जब रुकू से सर उठाते तो भी, और जब सज्दे से सर उठाते तो भी।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مالک بن حویرث رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم جب نماز میں داخل ہو جاتے …. پھر آگے انہوں نے اسی طرح کی حدیث ذکر کی، اس میں انہوں نے یہ اضافہ کیا ہے کہ جب آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم رکوع کرتے تو بھی ایسا ہی کرتے، اور جب رکوع سے سر اٹھاتے تو بھی، اور جب سجدہ سے سر اٹھاتے تو بھی ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik bin Huwairith (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) jab namaz mein dakhil ho jate... phir aagay unhone isi tarah ki hadith zikr ki, is mein unhone yeh izafa kiya hai ke jab aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ruku karte toh bhi aisa hi karte, aur jab ruku se sar uthate toh bhi, aur jab sajday se sar uthate toh bhi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1087]</p>

## Narration 650

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-nasai%3A1088)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">أَخْبَرَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عُبَيْدٍ الْكُوفِيُّ الْمُحَارِبِيُّ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ الْمُبَارَكِ، عَنْ مَعْمَرٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ سَالِمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَرْفَعُ يَدَيْهِ إِذَا افْتَتَحَ الصَّلاَةَ وَإِذَا رَكَعَ وَإِذَا رَفَعَ وَكَانَ لاَ يَفْعَلُ ذَلِكَ فِي السُّجُودِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1088]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1088" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Ibn &#x27;Umar said: &quot;The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) used to raise his hands when he started to pray, and when he bowed, and when he stood up, but he did not do that when he prostrated</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1088]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1088" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुम) कहते हैं के रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) नमाज़ शुरू करते, और जब रुकू करते, और जब रुकू से अपना सर उठाते, तो अपने दोनों हाथ उठाते और सज्दा करने में ऐसा नहीं करते थे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1088]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/nasai/1088" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہم کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نماز شروع کرتے، اور جب رکوع کرتے، اور جب رکوع سے اپنا سر اٹھاتے، تو اپنے دونوں ہاتھ اٹھاتے اور سجدہ کرنے میں ایسا نہیں کرتے تھے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1088]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhum) kehte hain ke Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) namaz shuru karte, aur jab ruku karte, aur jab ruku se apna sar uthate, toh apne dono hath uthate aur sajda karne mein aisa nahi karte thay.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan an-Nasa&#x27;i · 1088]</p>
